PASS  Public HTTPS endpoint - https://api.c2coffeeandcandle.com/health and https://api.c2coffeeandcandle.com/v1/health must be reachable through IIS.
PASS  Local API and database - The container health endpoint must confirm database connectivity.
PASS  SMTP transport - The live OTP mail transport must authenticate successfully; no message is sent.
PASS  Admin browser origin - At least one HTTPS CORS origin is required for the admin session cookie flow.
PASS  FCM configuration - FCM delivery is enabled and the service-account secret has the required fields; a physical device test remains required.
PASS  Top-up gateway policy - Top-up gateway chip allowlist is fpx, duitnow_qr. Brand-level activation is verified for every allowed method.
PASS  Financial reconciliation queue - No top-up refund or referral reversal may remain in the manual-review queue before release.
PASS  Voucher allocation integrity - Voucher concurrency migration, active allocations, and claimable tier rewards must be internally consistent.
PASS  Payment-service notice integrity - The scheduled-maintenance notice schema and stored windows must be internally consistent.
MANUAL  Verify one OTP email, Admin Web login/refresh/logout, and a real device notification.

MANUAL  Complete one end-to-end fpx/duitnow_qr payment and verified callback in CHIP before customer launch.
